=== PROMPT VARIANT ===
r1

=== PROMPT ===
You will be given a competitive programming problem.
Takahashi has 2N teeth: N upper teeth and N lower teeth.
The length of the i-th upper tooth from the left (1 \leq i \leq N) is U _ i, and the length of the i-th lower tooth from the left (1 \leq i \leq N) is D _ i.
His teeth are said to “fit together well” if both of the following conditions are satisfied:

- There exists an integer H such that U _ i + D _ i = H for every integer i with 1 \leq i \leq N.
- \lvert U _ i - U _ {i+1} \rvert \leq X for every integer i with 1 \leq i < N.

He can perform the following operation any number of times:

- Pay 1 yen to use a tooth-grinding machine, choose exactly one tooth whose length is positive, and reduce its length by 1.

No other method may be used to change the lengths of the teeth.
Find the minimum total amount of money he needs to pay to make his teeth fit together well.

Input

The input is given from Standard Input in the following format:
N X
U _ 1 D _ 1
U _ 2 D _ 2
\vdots
U _ N D _ N

Output

Print the answer.

Constraints


- 2 \leq N \leq 2 \times 10^5
- 1 \leq U _ i \leq 10^9 \ (1 \leq i \leq N)
- 1 \leq D _ i \leq 10^9 \ (1 \leq i \leq N)
- 1 \leq X \leq 10^9
- All input values are integers.

Sample Input 1

4 3
3 1
4 1
5 9
2 6

Sample Output 1

15

Initially, Takahashi’s teeth have the following lengths:

For example, you can make them fit together well in the following way:

It costs 15 yen to achieve these lengths.
It is impossible to make them fit together well with 14 yen or less, so print 15.

Sample Input 2

4 1000000000
3 3
3 3
3 3
3 3

Sample Output 2

0

It is possible that the teeth already fit together well without any changes.

Sample Input 3

4 1
1000000000 1000000000
1000000000 1000000000
1000000000 1000000000
1 1

Sample Output 3

5999999994

Note that the answer may exceed the 32-bit integer range.

Sample Input 4

15 128
748 169
586 329
972 529
432 519
408 587
138 249
656 114
632 299
984 755
404 772
155 506
832 854
353 465
387 374
567 385

Sample Output 4

9460

Reason in a commit-implement-recheck way.

A productive rhythm for this task usually looks like this:
- Isolate the decisive constraints, the target complexity, and at most one or two approaches that are still truly plausible.
- If two candidates remain, compare them only on the decisive constraint and commit quickly to the stronger one instead of keeping a wide search open.
- Before coding, keep only a tiny implementation ledger for the chosen plan: main invariant, key state or transition, and the one or two edge cases most likely to fail.
- Move into code as soon as that ledger is clear. After coding starts, keep reasoning attached to the current draft rather than reopening the high-level search.
- After the first complete draft, do one short implementation-check-adjust-check loop on the same draft: test the key edge case, patch locally if needed, and recheck.
- Reopen the main design only if a concrete counterexample breaks the core invariant. Otherwise prefer the local patch and keep closing.
- Once the current draft, invariant, and short recheck still agree, finalize the solution.

Try to avoid patterns like these:
- spending too long comparing nearby plans before committing to one workable approach;
- returning from code to broad algorithm discussion without a concrete contradiction;
- turning one local doubt into a full restart when the current draft only needs a small patch.

Output only the final Python code enclosed in a code block.

Let's micro-optimize ok:

def feasible(H):
    U_ = U; D_ = D; X_ = X; n_ = N
    l = H - D_[0]
    if l < 0:
        l = 0
    r = U_[0]
    if r > H:
        r = H
    a = l
    b = r
    for i in range(1, n_):
        l = H - D_[i]
        if l < 0:
            l = 0
        r = U_[i]
        if r > H:
            r = H
        t = a - X_
        if l > t:
            a = l
        else:
            a = t
        t = b + X_
        if r < t:
            b = r
        else:
            b = t
        if a > b:
            return False
    return True

Let's write code:

import sys

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, X = map(int, input().split())
    U = [0]*N
    D = [0]*N
    total = 0
    high = 10**30
    for i in range(N):
        u, d = map(int, input().split())
        U[i] = u
        D[i] = d
        total += u + d
        s = u + d
        if s < high:
            high = s

    def ok(H):
        u_list = U
        d_list = D
        x = X
        n = N
        l = H - d_list[0]
        if l < 0:
            l = 0
        r = u_list[0]
        if r > H:
            r = H
        a = l
        b = r
        # if a > b: return False
        for i in range(1, n):
            l = H - d_list[i]
            if l < 0:
                l = 0
            r = u_list[i]
            if r > H:
                r = H
            t = a - x
            if l > t:
                a = l
            else:
                a = t
            t = b + x
            if r < t:
                b = r
            else:
                b = t
            if a > b:
                return False
        return True

    lo = 0
    hi = high
    while lo < hi:
        mid = (lo + hi + 1) // 2
        if ok(mid):
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid - 1
    print(total - N * lo)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

def main():
    import sys
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, X = map(int, input().split())
    U = [0] * N
    D = [0] * N
    total = 0
    high = 10**30
    for i in range(N):
        u, d = map(int, input().split())
        U[i] = u
        D[i] = d
        total += u + d
        s = u + d
        if s < high:
            high = s

    def ok(H, U=U, D=D, X=X, n=N):
        l = H - D[0]
        if l < 0:
            l = 0
        r = U[0]
        if r > H:
            r = H
        a = l
        b = r
        if a > b:
            return False
        for i in range(1, n):
            l = H - D[i]
            if l < 0:
                l = 0
            r = U[i]
            if r > H:
                r = H
            t = a - X
            if l > t:
                a = l
            else:
                a = t
            t = b + X
            if r < t:
                b = r
            else:
                b = t
            if a > b:
                return False
        return True

    lo = 0
    hi = high
    while lo < hi:
        mid = (lo + hi + 1) // 2
        if ok(mid):
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid - 1
    print(total - N * lo)

This is good.

=== ANSWER ===
```python
import sys

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N, X = map(int, input().split())

    U = [0] * N
    D = [0] * N
    total = 0
    high = 10**30

    for i in range(N):
        u, d = map(int, input().split())
        U[i] = u
        D[i] = d
        total += u + d
        s = u + d
        if s < high:
            high = s

    def ok(H, U=U, D=D, X=X, n=N):
        l = H - D[0]
        if l < 0:
            l = 0
        r = U[0]
        if r > H:
            r = H

        a = l
        b = r
        if a > b:
            return False

        for i in range(1, n):
            l = H - D[i]
            if l < 0:
                l = 0
            r = U[i]
            if r > H:
                r = H

            t = a - X
            if l > t:
                a = l
            else:
                a = t

            t = b + X
            if r < t:
                b = r
            else:
                b = t

            if a > b:
                return False

        return True

    lo = 0
    hi = high

    while lo < hi:
        mid = (lo + hi + 1) // 2
        if ok(mid):
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid - 1

    print(total - N * lo)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```
